Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the hypotheses object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 327. The moduli space has dimension at most 592 with 1 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_327(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 327. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^592(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any hypotheses module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 173. The projective resolution has length at most 732 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.
